# NFL Prop Engine Feature Dictionary

This dictionary is generated against the canonical historical feature contract and includes every canonical production feature plus model-local efficiency features persisted in model manifests.

**Coverage contract:** the canonical source of truth is `data/historical/features/feature_manifest.json`. The canonical table currently contains **774** features. Seven additional `eff_*` features are materialized only inside efficiency-model inference/training, so this document contains **781** unique documented feature names.

`positions_used` and `targets_used` describe applicability. Actual consumption is additionally gated by each persisted component, efficiency, or direct-model `feature_manifest.json`; a feature's presence in the canonical table does not imply every target model consumes it.

All lag rules are subordinate to the global rule: **no realized source observation at or after the target game's kickoff may enter the target row.**

## Field definitions

- `feature_name`: exact persisted model/canonical feature name.
- `feature_family`: canonical or model-local feature family.
- `data_type`: numeric/binary/categorical production type.
- `source_file`: immediate Prop Engine source contract.
- `source_column_or_formula`: direct column or deterministic transformation.
- `lag_rule`: pregame/as-of rule.
- `positions_used`: position applicability, with final gating by target/model manifest.
- `targets_used`: target applicability, with final gating by persisted model manifest.
- `missing_value_rule`: approved null/prior/fallback behavior.
- `leakage_risk`: principal way the feature could become post-kickoff or target-contaminated.
